Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the harmonic space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 694 and the convergence rate is O(187/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_694(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 694. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^187(S, F) is trivial. QED.
